Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any regular module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 317. The projective resolution has length at most 118 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_317(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 317. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^118(S, F) is trivial. QED.
